4. Return On Investment (ROI): The ratio of revenue generated to the total investment made in the app.

It's important to note that these metrics may not be directly comparable across different types of apps or markets. Additionally, the ARPU can fluctuate based on changes in user behavior and engagement with the app.

1. **Sentencing Justice**: The death penalty provides a clear and visible punishment for crimes, which can deter people from committing similar offenses. This sends a message that the consequences of breaking the law are severe.

2. **Involuntary Sentencing**: In some cases, the death penalty is used as an alternative to life imprisonment or other forms of incarceration. This means that individuals who commit serious crimes may be sentenced to death rather than being executed, which can reduce the likelihood of reoffending.

3. **Community Support**: The death penalty has been shown to increase community support for victims of crime. When someone is convicted of a crime, they feel a sense of justice and closure, which can help them to seek help and support from others.

4. **Rape Victim Protection**: Some countries use the death penalty to protect rape victims by serving them with sentences that include life imprisonment. This can provide a deterrent effect on future crimes committed by rapists.

5. **Cultural Values**: The death penalty has also been seen as a symbol of cultural values and norms, such as respect for the dead and the sanctity of human life. This can influence public opinion and behavior towards crime prevention.

LORA MODEL ANSWER (LoRA ON):

Additionally, the death penalty provides a sense of justice and closure for victims and their loved ones, which can help reduce feelings of hopelessness and despair associated with crime. Furthermore, the death penalty can also serve as a form of social control, ensuring that individuals who commit serious offenses receive severe consequences rather than being punished for minor mistakes or errors. Overall, the effectiveness of the death penalty in reducing crime is based on its ability to deter crime and provide a sense of justice and closure to victims and society at large.
